PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS audio_features;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS track_artists;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS tracks;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS genres;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS albums;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS artists;

CREATE TABLE artists (
    artist_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    artist_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
        CHECK (length(trim(artist_name)) > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE albums (
    album_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    album_name TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (length(trim(album_name)) > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE genres (
    genre_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    genre_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
        CHECK (length(trim(genre_name)) > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE tracks (
    track_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    track_name TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (length(trim(track_name)) > 0),
    album_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    release_date TEXT NOT NULL
        CHECK (date(release_date) IS NOT NULL),
    genre_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    duration_ms INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (duration_ms > 0),
    popularity INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (popularity BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
    explicit INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (explicit IN (0, 1)),
    FOREIGN KEY (album_id) REFERENCES albums(album_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (genre_id) REFERENCES genres(genre_id)
);

CREATE TABLE track_artists (
    track_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    artist_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    artist_position INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (artist_position >= 1),
    PRIMARY KEY (track_id, artist_id),
    UNIQUE (track_id, artist_position),
    FOREIGN KEY (track_id) REFERENCES tracks(track_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY (artist_id) REFERENCES artists(artist_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE TABLE audio_features (
    track_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    danceability REAL NOT NULL CHECK (danceability BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    energy REAL NOT NULL CHECK (energy BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    musical_key INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (musical_key BETWEEN 0 AND 11),
    loudness REAL NOT NULL,
    mode INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN (0, 1)),
    speechiness REAL NOT NULL CHECK (speechiness BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    acousticness REAL NOT NULL CHECK (acousticness BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    instrumentalness REAL NOT NULL CHECK (instrumentalness BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    liveness REAL NOT NULL CHECK (liveness BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    valence REAL NOT NULL CHECK (valence BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    tempo REAL NOT NULL CHECK (tempo >= 0),
    time_signature INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (time_signature BETWEEN 0 AND 7),
    FOREIGN KEY (track_id) REFERENCES tracks(track_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- ПРОВЕРОЧНЫЕ ЗАПРОСЫ
-- Ожидается: artists=992, albums=1249, genres=66, tracks=1500, track_artists=2089, audio_features=1500.
-- PRAGMA foreign_key_check должен вернуть пустой результат.
SELECT 'artists' AS table_name, COUNT(*) AS row_count FROM artists
UNION ALL SELECT 'albums', COUNT(*) FROM albums
UNION ALL SELECT 'genres', COUNT(*) FROM genres
UNION ALL SELECT 'tracks', COUNT(*) FROM tracks
UNION ALL SELECT 'track_artists', COUNT(*) FROM track_artists
UNION ALL SELECT 'audio_features', COUNT(*) FROM audio_features;

PRAGMA foreign_key_check;


--Базовая выборка
--SELECT track_name, explicit, popularity FROM tracks
--ORDER BY popularity;
--Агрегирование
--Кол-во треков:
--SELECT COUNT(track_name) as count_tracks FROM tracks
--Агрегирование: max, min, avg popularity, где explicit=0/1
--SELECT min(popularity) AS min_popularity, max(popularity) AS max_popularity, avg(popularity) AS avg_popularity, explicit AS where_explicit FROM tracks
--WHERE explicit=0;
--SELECT min(popularity) AS min_popularity, max(popularity) AS max_popularity, avg(popularity) AS avg_popularity, explicit AS where_explicit FROM tracks
--WHERE explicit=1;
SELECT
    genres.genre_name AS genre,
    tracks.explicit,
    COUNT(tracks.track_id) AS tracks_count,
    ROUND(AVG(tracks.popularity), 2) AS avg_popularity,
    MIN(tracks.popularity) AS min_popularity,
    MAX(tracks.popularity) AS max_popularity
FROM tracks 
INNER JOIN genres ON tracks.genre_id = genres.genre_id
GROUP BY
    genres.genre_name,
    tracks.explicit;
SELECT tracks.track_name,
    artists.artist_name,
    genres.genre_name,
    tracks.popularity,
    tracks.explicit
FROM tracks
JOIN genres ON genres.genre_id = tracks.genre_id
JOIN track_artists ON track_artists.track_id = tracks.track_id
JOIN artists ON artists.artist_id = track_artists.artist_id
GROUP BY tracks.track_id, tracks.track_name, genres.genre_name, tracks.popularity, tracks.explicit
ORDER BY tracks.popularity DESC, tracks.track_name
LIMIT 10;
SELECT explicit, avg(duration_ms)
FROM tracks
GROUP BY explicit;
